	type plug

		pcm "hw:APE,0"
		channels 2
		rate 48000
	}
	hint.description "Tegra APE Soundcard"

	type hw
	card APE
}

	type plug

		pcm "dmix:APE,0"
		channels 2
		rate 48000
	}
